1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/sar+b+ba ta thA ga ta tat+tA saM gra ha nA ma ma hA yA na sU tra/bod skad du/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi de kho na nyid bsdus pa zhes bya ba chen po'i mgon 
1b2(rgyud): po dang po/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das rnams bde stong mdzad 
1b3(rgyud): thugs rje chen po de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdo rje'i byin gyis brlabs dang /dam tshig gi ye shes rnam pa sna tshogs kyi khyad par dang ldan pa/de bzhin gshegs pa 
1b4(rgyud): thams cad kyi rin po che'i dbu rgyan gyis khams gsum gyi chos kyi rgyal po dbang bskur ba mnga' brnyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes rnal 'byor 
1b5(rgyud): chen po'i dbang phyug/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cid kyi phyag rgya thams cad mnyam pa nyid du thugs su chud cing /thams cad kyi dgos pa mdzad pa nyid kyis/sems can 
2a1(rgyud): @#//gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thams cad kyi re ba yongs su skong ba'i 'phrin las mdzad pa/thugs rje che ba rnam par snang mdzad dus gsum du rtag par bzhugs shing /sku dang /gsung dang /thugs thams cad rdo rje lta bur gyur 
2a2(rgyud): ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bzhugs shing bsngags bstod pa/nor bu dang rin po che chen pos spras pa//kha dog sna tshogs shing dril bu btags pa/dar dang /'phan dang /me tog gi phreng ba dang /rnga ma dang /dra ba 
2a3(rgyud): dang /dra ba phyed pa dang /zla ba rlung gis bskyod pa rnams kyis mdzes par byas pa'i 'og min gyi lha'i rgyal po'i gnas na bzhugs pa/byang chub sems dpa' bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa dgu la 'di lta ste/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje dang /
2a4(rgyud): kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po dang /nam mkha'i snying po dang /rdo rje khu tshur dang /'jam dpal dang /sems bskyed ma thag tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /nam mkha'i mdzod dang /bdud kyi stobs thams cad rab tu 
2a5(rgyud): 'joms pa la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' semsdpa' chen po bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa dag dang /gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams dang thabs cig tu bzhugs te/de yang 'di ltar 'dzam bu'i gling na de bzhin gshegs pa 
2a6(rgyud): tshad med grangs med pa de dag gi skus kyang til gyi gang bu bzhin du gang bar snang ngo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku re re las kyang sangs rgyas kyi zhing grangs med tshad med pa rnams snang ngo//sangs rgyas kyi zhing de dag na yang chos kyi tshul 'di dag 'chad 
2b1(rgyud): do//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen po/nam mkha'i dbyings thams cad bzhin du rtag par bzhugs shing /sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje lta bur gyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang mnyam pa nyid du 'jug pas/rdo rje dbyings 
2b2(rgyud): thams cad thugs su chud pa'i ye shes sems dpa' nam mkha'i dbyings thams can gyi rdul shin tu phra mo rdo rje'i byin gyis brlabs las byung ba'i ye shes kyi snying po/de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas pa nyid kyi rdo rje chen po'i ye shes rin po che'i dbang bskur ba'i rin po che chen 
2b3(rgyud): po/nam mkha'i dbyings tham sa cad du spros pa'i de bzhin nyid kyi ye shes mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pas byang chub par gyur cing /de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi ngo bo nyid dag pa'i phyir chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin dag par thugs su chud pa/nam mkha'i 
2b4(rgyud): khams thams cad du khyab pa'i gzugs thams cad du yang dag par ston pa/mkhyen pas sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i khams rnams gdul bar spyod pa'i mchog tu gyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bka' nod cing don yod par mdzad pa 
2b5(rgyud): nyid kyis thams cad dang mnyam pa med cing bla na med pa'i 'phrin las thams cad mdzad pa/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams cad kyi byang chub chen por sems brtan pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mtha' yas pa thams cad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig dang /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad 
2b6(rgyud): mnyes par bya ba'i ye shes kyi dbang phyug dang /de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyis legs so zhes bya ba dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi rin po che'i dbang chen po bskur ba dang /de bzhin gsheg ba thams cad kyi nyi ma'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor dang /de 
2b7(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi bsam pa'i rgyal po yid bzhin gyi nor bu'i rgyal po'i rin po che'i dpal dang /de bzhin gshegasab thams cad kyi bzhad pa chen po dang /de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyi dag pa'i chos chen po dang /de bzhin bshegs pa thams 
3a1(rgyud): @#//cad kyi shes rab chen po'i ye shes dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo dang /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi dgongs pa 'chad pa dang /de bzhin gshegasab thams cad kyi gdon mi za bar thams cad kyi dgongs ba mdzad pa dang /
3a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi brtson 'grus chen po'i go cha shin tu brtan po dang /de bzhin gshegasapa thams cad kyis srung zhing skyob pa'i gnod sbyin dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje'i phyag rgya bcings pa'i ye shes 
3a3(rgyud): rnams so//kun bzang rab tu gdon mi za//bdud dang rab dgyes gtso bo dang //mkha' skyes shin tu gzi chen dang //rin chen dpal dang 'dzum pa che//kun tu gzigs pa'i dbang chen dang //'jam dpal dkyil 'khor thams cad dang //mi gsung ba dang 
3a4(rgyud): thams cad las//brtson 'grus ma rungs dam du gzung //rdo rje lcags kyu mda' gzhu dang //rin chen nyi ma rgyal mtshan 'dzum//pad+ma ral gri 'khor lo gsung //las dang go cha 'jigs byed bcings//thog ma tha ma med cing zhi//ma rungs khro bo bzod pa che//
3a5(rgyud): gnod sbyin srin po dam pa brtan//gzi chen dpa' bo dbang po che//u ma'i bdag po skye dgu'i mgon//gtogs 'dod rgyal 'dod thub pa che//'jig rten skyong dang mkha' dang sa//'jig rten gsum dang khams gsum pa//'byung chen sems can don rab mdzad//
3a6(rgyud): thams cad gu lang tshangs pa dang //'khor dang mya ngan 'da' dang rtag/yang dag 'tsho dang mchod pa che//sangs rgyas dag pa theg pa che//srid gsum rtag pa'ang de yin no//'jig rten gsum rgyal ba de spyod dang //bde spyod gtso bo rab 'dul mdzad//rdo rje gtso dang 
3a7(rgyud): sa rab mchog/ye shes pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par thar//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi spyod//sangs rgyas don dang sangs rgyas thugs//byang chub kun dang bla na med//rnam par snang mdzad rgyal ba'i gtso//rang byung gzungs dang dran pa 
3b1(rgyud): dang //sems dpa' che dang phyag rgya che//ting 'dzin sangs rgyas 'phrin las mdzod//yang dag sangs rgyas kun bdag nyid//yod pa rtag don rtogs mdzad pa//sdong po chen po nag po che//'dod chags chen po bde ba che//thabs chen dang ni mchog chen mchog/kun 
3b2(rgyud): gyi mchog dang gnas dbang po//bcom ldan byang chub chen po'i thugs//byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs la gnas so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'di dper na til gyi gang 
3b3(rgyud): bu bzhin du yongs su gang bar gyur to//de nas de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad 'dus te byang chub sems dpa' chen pa don thams cad grub pa zhes bya ba byang chub kyi snying por bzhugs pa gang na ba der gshegs te/byang chub sems dpa' de la ched du rdzogs par longs spyod 
3b4(rgyud): pa'i sku bstan nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rigs kyi bu khyod kyis gang gi phyir de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi de kho na nyid mngon par ma rtogs par dka' ba spyod pa thams cad la spro ba bskyed cing /ji ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon 
3b5(rgyud): par rdzogs par bya snyam/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po don thams cad grub pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bskul bas mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs te/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad la phyag 'tshal nas 'di skad ces 
3b6(rgyud): gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de kho na nyid ni ji lta bu lags/ji ltar na bsgrub par bgyi bka' stsal bar gsol/de skad ces gsol pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa de dag thams cad kyis gsung gcig tu 'di skad ces bka' stsal
3b7(rgyud): to//rigs kyi bu rang gi sems la rtog cing mnyam par zhog la rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngags 'di ci dgar zlos shing bsgrub par gyis shig/oM tsit+ta pa ti bi d+hi Sha ro me/de nas byang chub sems pa'i don thams cad grub pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad la 'di skad ces gsol 
4a1(rgyud): @#//
4b1(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' chen po don thams cad grub pa de la/rdo rje'i dbyings/rdo rje'i dbyings zhes bya ba'i ming gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje dbyings kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa de dag 
4b2(rgyud): thams cad la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnams/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cadakyi sku ni bdag legs par mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis bka' stsal pa/sems dpa' chen po de lta bas na/sems 
4b3(rgyud): dpa'i rdo rje de rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas kyi skur bdag nyid rang bzhin gyis grub pa'i sngags ci dga' bar zlos la sgoms shig/oM ma thA sar+b+ba ta thA ga ta sta thA a haM/de nas de skad ces bka' stsal pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen pa rdo 
4b4(rgyud): rje'i dbyings kyis bdag nyid de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams tsad la phyag byas te 'di skad ces gsol to/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad kyis bdag byin gyis rlobs la mngon par 
4b5(rgyud): rdzogs par byang chub pa 'di brtan par mdzod cig/de nas de skad ces gsol pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje dbyings kyi sems dpa'i rdo rje der zhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje'i dbyings kyis skad 
4b6(rgyud): cig ma de nyid kyi tshe na/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes mngon par rdzogs par sang rgyas so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rdo rje mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shesakyi phyag rgya gsang ba'i dam tshig tu zhugs so//de bzhin gshegs ba thams 
4b7(rgyud): cad kyi rdo rje rin po che'i dbang bskur ba mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi phyag rgya gsang ba'i dam chog tu zhugs so//de bzhinagashegs pa thams cad kyi chos mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shesartogs pa rang bzhin gyis dag go/de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad kyi thams cad mnyam pa 
5a1(rgyud): @#//nyid rang bzhin gyis 'od gsal zhing ye shes kyi 'byung gnas su gyur pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas su gyur to//de nas yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sems dpa'i rdo rje de las de bzhin gshegs pa thams
5a2(rgyud): cad byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes bskyed/nam mkha' las byung ba'i nor bu rin po che chen po'i dbang bskur bas dbang bskur ro//kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i chos kyi ye shes bskyed/de bzhin gshegs 
5a3(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi 'phrin las thams cad la bzhag nas/ri rab kyi rtse mo gang na pa dang /khang bu brtsegs pa rtse mo rdo rje nor bu dang rin po che las byas pa gang na ba der gshegs te byon nas de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje dbyings de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad du byin gyis brlabs 
5a4(rgyud): nas dbyer med par gyur te/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi seng ge'i gdan la zhal kun tu bstan te bzhugs par gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa mi bskyod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rin chen 'byung ldan dang /de bzhin gshegs ba spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang
5a5(rgyud): po'i rgyal po dang /de bzhin gshegs gdon mi za bar grub pa rnams/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad du so so nas bdag nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs te/bcom ldan 'das shAkya thub pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad mnyam pa nyid du rab tu rtogs pa'i 
5a6(rgyud): phyir/phyogs thams cad mnyam pa nyid du dmigs nas phyogs bzhir bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas nas ring po ma lon pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad kyi thugs kun tu bzang 
5a7(rgyud): po zhes bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi nam mkha' las byung ba'i nor bu rin po che chen po'i dbang gis dbang bskur ba/de bzhin gshegs pa pa thams cad kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i chos kyi ye shes dam pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pa/de bzhin
5b1(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'phrin las thams cad gdon mi za bar ston pa/thogs pa med pa/dgongs pa rdzogs pa/yid kyi bsam pa'o cog yongs su rdzogs pas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad du bdag nyid kyis byin gyis brlabs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
5b2(rgyud): cad kyi byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po zhes bya ba'i dam tshig las byung ba/sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi theg pa chen po mngon par rtogs pa zhes bya ba'i de 
5b3(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra satwa/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs ka nas phyung ma thag pa 'di ni/bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang po de nyid zla ba'i dkyil 'khor du gyur nas byung ste/sems 
5b4(rgyud): can thams cad kyi byang chub kyi sems chen po yang dag par phyung ba nas de bzhin gshegs ba thams chad kyi phyogs phyogs su 'khod do//de nas zla ba'i dkyil 'khor de dag las kyang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi rdo rje byung ste/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad 
5b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa'i thugs su zhugs par gyur to//rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu brtan pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas nam mkha'i khams thams cad du 'od zer rtse lnga pa'i phreng bas khyab par 
5b6(rgyud): zhugs te de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje lta bu'i dbyings su gyur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs ka nas byung ste phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje de las rdo rje de lta bu'i 'od zer gzugs dang kha dog sna tshogs pa/
5b7(rgyud): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du snang bas khyab par byed pa rnams byung ngo //rdo rje lta bu'i 'od zer de dag gi rtse mo las 'jig rten gyi khams thams chad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo steng kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku byung ste chos kyi dbyings kun tu khyab pa thams cad dang /nam 
6a1(rgyud): @#//mkha'i khams kyi mthas gtugs pa thams cad dang /'jig rten gyi khams rab 'byam rgya mtsho'i sprin thams cad du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes mngon par byang chub pas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi byang chub kyi sems 
6a2(rgyud): chen po bskyed pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa sna tshogs pa bsgrub pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rigs mnyes par mdzad pa dang /byang chub kyi snying po chen por gshegs pa dang /bdud thams cad 'dul ba dang /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi 
6a3(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid kyi byang chub chen por mngon par byed chub pa dang /dam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba nas/ji srid sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams yongs su skyob cing thams cad la phan pa dang /bde ba mdzad pa dang /de bzhin gsheg pa 
6a4(rgyud): thams cad kyi ye shes dang mngon par shes pa dam pa'i dngos grub bsgrub pa la sogs pa de bzhin gshegs pa thamasas cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams kyi bar du bstan nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni kun tu bzang po yin pa dang /shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig 
6a5(rgyud): tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/'di skad du ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o bdag ni rang byung ba'i//kun tu bzang po sems dpa' 
6a6(rgyud): brtan//de ni brtan phyir lus min yang //sems dpa'i lus su gyur pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen bo kun tu bzang po'i sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/
6a7(rgyud): bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ting nge 'dzin dang ye shes kyi dam tshig rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi tshul khrims dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /
6b1(rgyud): shes rab dang /rnam par grol ba dang /rnam par grol pa'i ye shes mthong ba dang /chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba dang /sems can gyi don bya ba dang /thabs chen po dang /stobs dang /brtson 'grus dang /ye shes chen po'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma 
6b2(rgyud): lus shing lus pa med pa rnams yongs su bskyab pa dang /thams cad kyi dbang por bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/ji srid de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dang mngon par shes pa bla na med pa dang /theg pa chen 
6b3(rgyud): po mngon par rtogs pa grub pa'i mchog thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po de/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'khor los sgyur ba nyid du sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku rin po che'i dbu rgyan dang /cod pan gyi 
6b4(rgyud): dbang gis dbang bskur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi grub pa'o rdo rje de de'i lag par byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyis/lag na rdo rje lag na rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang shes dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 
6b5(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje phyag g.yon pa rdo rje bskyings par bzhugs te/phyag g.yas pas rdo rje thugs kar gsor zhing 'phen pa'i tshul du 'dzin cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi grub pa yi//rdo rje bla na med pa 'di//bdag ste bdag gi lag 
6b6(rgyud): tu byin//rdo rje la ni rdo rje gnas//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gdon mi za ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
6b7(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan drang ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra rA dza/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs ka nas byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyid 
7a1(rgyud): @#//
7b1(rgyud): pa rdo rje dgug pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje dgug pa rdo rje lugs kyu des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan 'dren cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi rdo rje yi//
7b2(rgyud): ye shes bla na med pa 'di//sangs rgyas kun gyi don grub phyir//kun nas spyan drang dam pa'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' chen po bdud ces bya ba'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpas byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par 
7b3(rgyud): zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams ces mnyes par byed pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra rA ga/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thug pa las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 
7b4(rgyud): 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi me tog gi mdar gyur nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas rdo rje'i mda' chen po'i dbyibs su gyur te phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas 
7b5(rgyud): rdo rje mda'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par bya ba la sogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/
7b6(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin shin tu bdud byed pa dang rab tu brtan pa'i phyir/gcig tu 'dres par gyur na/byang chub sems dpa' chen po bdud bya bar gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam pa snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o 
7b7(rgyud): bdag ni rang bzhin dag/rang byung ba dag dgyes byed pa//'dod chags bral rnams dag bya'i phyir//'dod chags kyis ni 'dul bar byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen//po bdud de'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
8a1(rgyud): @#//cad kyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs so//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad mnyes par byed pa'i zin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/
8a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi bdud rdo rje dam tshig dang sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams mgu bar bya ba dang /thams cad kyis bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
8a3(rgyud): cad kyi bdud kyi mchog grub pa thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje'i mda' de byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bdud ces bya ba de'i phyag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo rje'i gzhu rdo rje'i gzhu zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 
8a4(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i gzhu zhes bya bas rdo rje'i mda' des de bzhin gshegs pa thamad cad la bdud byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas sman gyi 'dod chags kyi//ye shes 'di ni dri ma med//'dod chags kyis ni chags bral bcom//bde ba 
8a5(rgyud): thams cad sbyin par byed//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis yang byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i sems dpa'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 
8a6(rgyud): thams cad kyi rab tu dga' ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //pad ma hUM/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de legs so 
8a7(rgyud): zhes bya bar gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs su gyur te phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyangs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra 
8b1(rgyud): mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis legs so zhes bya ba la sogs pa dang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu dga' bar byed pa nyid dang /
8b2(rgyud): rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po'i lus su gyur te/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas par gyur nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du 
8b3(rgyud): brjod do//e ma'o thams cad mkhyen kun gyi//legs byin nang na bdag mchog pas//rnam rtog bral ba de dag gi//nges par dgyes pa bskyed pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po rab du dga' ba'i rgyal po'i sku de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung 
8b4(rgyud): ste/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi rgyab na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas/yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin bshegs pa thams cad mnyes par byed pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 
8b5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rab tu dgyes pa bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi dam tshig dang sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i sems can thams cad mgu bar bya ba dang /thams cad bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
8b6(rgyud): cad kyi dgyes pa'i ro bla na med pa dam pa grub pa 'thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje dgyes pa de byang chub sems dpa' chenapo rab tu dga' ba'i rgyal po de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rnams cad kyis rdo rje dgyes pa rdo rje dgyes pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming 
8b7(rgyud): gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje dgyes pas rdo rje dgyes pa'i dbyibs des de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad la legs so zhes bya bas rab tu dgyes par byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas 
9a1(rgyud): @#//kun gyi legs sbyin pa//'di ni rab tu 'jug byed pas//rdo rje thams cad dgyes par byed//lha rdzas dgyes pa spel ba'o//byang chub kyi sems chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad spyan drang ba'i dam tshig dang /de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad 
9a2(rgyud): mnyes par byed pa'i ye shes dang /dgyes pa chen po dang 'di bzhi ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dam tshig chen po'i sems dpa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chu chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i dam tshig las byung ba/rin po 
9a3(rgyud): che'i byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis dbang bskur ba'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas byung ngo//badzra ra bla/de nas de bzhin gshegs 
9a4(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin thams cad nam mkha' dang mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes shin tu rtogs pa yin pa'i phyir/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de nam mkha' thams cad kyi 'od zer du gyur nas byung ngo//nam mkha' 
9a5(rgyud): thams cad kyi 'od zer de dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad snang bar byas pas thams cad nam mkha'i dbyings lta bur gyur to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis nam mkha'i dbyings de dag thams cad bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang 
9a6(rgyud): mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge'adzin shin tu bsgoms par gyur pa'i phyir yang nam mkha'i khams thams cad las byung ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad du mnyam par 'jug pa'i tshad tsam gyi rdo rje rin po che chen po'i dbyibs su gyur nas de bzhin gshegs 
9a7(rgyud): pa'i phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje rin po che'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rdul phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi skur gyur nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dbang bskur ba la sogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs 
9b1(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du byas nas rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi snying po las shin tu byung ba dang /rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' sems 
9b2(rgyud): dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snying po'i skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o chags med rgyal ba yang //khams gsum dbang por dgongs pas na//rdo rje rin chen dam pa yi//dbang 
9b3(rgyud): gi mchog ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po nam mkha'i snyid po'i lus de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur 
9b4(rgyud): to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi nor bu rin po che'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing 
9b5(rgyud): lus pa med pa'i don thams cad yongs su grub par bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang /yid bde ba skyong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs par thams cad kyi don dam pa phun sum tshogs pa grub pa thob pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 
9b6(rgyud): nam mkha'i snying po rdo rje rin po che'i 'khor lo bskor bar rdo rje rin po che'i myu gu'i dbang gis dbang bskur nas lag tu rdo rje'i nor bu de byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyis rdo rje snying po rdo rje snying po zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gi dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub 
9b7(rgyud): sems dpa' sems dpa' che na rdo rje'i snying pos rdo rje'i nor bu de rang gis dbang bskur ba'i gnas su 'jog cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi sems dpa'i dbyibs//dbang bskur de ni bdag yin te//bdag gi lag tu byin pas na//rin 
10a1(rgyud): @#//chen la ni rin chen bzhag/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzi brjid chen po zhes bya ba'i dam tshig las byung ba'i rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin 
10a2(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra te dza/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das phyag na rdo rje de nyid de/nyi 
10a3(rgyud): ma'i dkyil 'khor chen por gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje nyi ma'i dbyibs su gyur te bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor de las 'jig 
10a4(rgyud): rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi 'od zer 'gyed pa la sogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/
10a5(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu gzi brjid che ba nyid dang rab tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur na/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzi brjid chen po'i skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'dis rnam par snang mdzad kyis thugs la gnas 
10a6(rgyud): te|ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o sems can khams kun du//snang byed gzi brjid dpe med pa dag/gang phyir sangs rgyas dag pa yi//'dren pa rnams kyang sbyor bar byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po dri ma med pa'i gzi 
10a7(rgyud): brjid de'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thamams cad kyi g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas te/bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od 
10b1(rgyud): kyi dkyil 'khor gyi byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi gzi brjid mtshungs pa med pa dang thams cad kyi 
10b2(rgyud): bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi 'od 'thob cing dngos grub dam pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir/rdo rje nyi ma de byang chub sems dpa' gzi brjid chen po de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad 
10b3(rgyud): kyis rdo rje'i 'od/rdo rje'i 'od ces bya ba'i rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje nyi ma des de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad la snang bar byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi snang ba 
10b4(rgyud): 'di//mi shis mun nag 'joms byed pa//shin tu phra rdul ji snyed pa'i//nyi ma'i 'od pas shin tu lhag/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' se mas dpa' chen po rin po che dpal gyi dam tshig las byung ba/rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i 
10b5(rgyud): rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dgongs pa yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba/de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra ke tu/de nas 
10b6(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi thugs ka las byung ma thag pa 'di ni/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de rgyal mtshan kha dog dang gzugs dang rgyan dang dbyibs sna tshogs par gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te/
10b7(rgyud): gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyi dbyibs su gyur nas phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyi dbyibs/de las/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de 
11a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rin po che'i rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba la sogs bsangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rin po che'i dpal dang shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/
11a2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' rin po che'i dpal gyi skur gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o don kun grub pa dang //dgongs pa thams cad rdzogs pa yi//don rnams thams cad 
11a3(rgyud): rdzogs byed pa'i//'dra med dpal ni bdag yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal gyi sku bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs ka nas byung ste de bzhin gshegs pa thams cadakyi g.yon phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang 
11a4(rgyud): bka' nod cing 'dug par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi rgyal mtshan gyis byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi nor bu yid bzhin gyi 
11a5(rgyud): rgyal po'i rgyal mtshan bsgreng ba'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi re ba thams cad yongs su bkang bar bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs ba thams cad kyi don chen po'i 
11a6(rgyud): dngos grub dam pa 'thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir rdo rje rgyal mtshan de byang chub sems dpa' chen po rin po che'i dpal de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan/rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan zhes rdo rje'i ming gi 
11a7(rgyud): dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje rgyal mtshan gyis rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan des/de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyor du 'chug nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas 
11b1(rgyud): kun gyi dgongs pa kun//yongs su rdzogs par byed pa yi//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal mtshan ni//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po'i dam tshig 
11b2(rgyud): las byung ba rin po che'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dgyes pa'i dam tshig ces bya ba de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra hAs/de 
11b3(rgyud): nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de/de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi 'dzum par gyur te byung nas/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur 
11b4(rgyud): nas rdo rje 'dzum pa'i dbyibs su gyur te/phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje 'dzum pa'i dbyings de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung nas/de bzhin gshegs ma thams cad kyi rmad 
11b5(rgyud): du byung ba la sogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rtag tu dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po yin pa dang shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur te/byang chub sems 
11b6(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po rtag tu dga' zhing rab tumagu ba'i dbang po'i lus su gyur nas/bcom ldan 'das rna ma par snang mdzad kyi thug la gnas te/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//rtag tu mnyam par bzhag rnams kyis//sangs rgyas don la rab sbyor 
11b7(rgyud): phyir//e ma'o bdag ni kun mchog gi//bzhad chen rmad du byung ba yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rtog par dga' zhing rab tu mgu pa'i dbang po'i lus bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs las byung nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad 
12a1(rgyud): @#//kyi rgyab kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rmad du byung ba'i byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/
12a2(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi rmad du byung ba bskyed pa'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa thams cad kyi dbang po bla na med pa bya ba dang /thams cad bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams
12a3(rgyud): cad kyi dbang po yongs su sbyong ba'i ye shes mngon par thob pa'i bar du 'bras bu de dag gi phyir byang chub sems dpa' chen po rtag par dga' zhing rab tu mgu ba'i dbang po de'i lag tu rdo rje 'dzum pa de de bzhin du byin no//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis rdo
12a4(rgyud): rje dga' ba/rdo rje dga' ba zhes rdo rje'i ming gi dbang gis dbang bskur ro//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rdo rje'i dga' ba rdo rje 'dzum pa des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dgyes par byed cing ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//'di ni sangs rgyas
12a5(rgyud): thams cad kyi//rmad byung skyed pa ston pa po//dgyes chen byed pa'i ye shes te//gtsug lag gzhan gyis shes mi nus//dbang bskur ba chen po dang 'od kyi dkyil 'khor 'dom gang ba dang sems dpa' chen po'i don dang dgyes pa chen po dang 'di bzhi ni de bzhin    
12a6(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyi dbang chen po'i sems dpa'o//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po'i dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyis brlabs rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par    
12a7(rgyud): zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi chos kyi dam tshig ces bya bde bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo//badzra d+har+m+ma/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje   
12b1(rgyud): 'dzin pa de nyid de/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni rang bzhin gyis dag pa'i chos mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rab tu rtogs pa'i phyir dam pa'i chos kyi 'od zer du gyur te byung ngo //dam pa'i chos kyi 'od zer de dag gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kun du snang bar byas pas 
12b2(rgyud): chos kyi dbyings lta bur gyur to//chos kyi dbyings de dag thams cad kyang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/nam mkha' thams cad dang mnyam par 'jug pa'i tshad tsam gyi rdo rje pad+ma chen po'i dbyibs su gyur te phyag gnyis 
12b3(rgyud): su gnas so//de nas rdo rje pad+ma'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ting nge 'dzin dang ye shes dang mngon par shes pa la sogs pa dang /
12b4(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thamad cad khyab par byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni kun tu spyan ras gzigs kyi dbang po yin pa dang /shin tu brtan pa yin pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur pas/byang chub 
12b5(rgyud): sems dpa' chen po kun tu spyan ras gzigs gyi dbang po'i skur gyur te/bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs la gnas nas/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//me 'thor dbyung gnod nas dag/dam pa'i don ni bdag yin te//gang phyir 
12b6(rgyud): gzigs 'dra'i chos rnams las//rnam par dag pa dmigs par gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun du spyan ras gzigs kyi lus de bcom ldan 'das rnam par bsnang mdzad kyi thugs las byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyan 
12b7(rgyud): snga na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas te bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ting nge 'dzin mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes dam tshig las byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de 
13a1(rgyud): @#//bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rnam par sbyang ba'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi bdag dang gzhan rnam par dag par bya ba dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang yid bde ba myong bar bya ba nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams

13a4(rgyud): kha phye bas/'dod chags rnam par dag cing ma chags pa'i ngo bo nyid du gzigs pas gzigs shing /ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'dod chags kyi//de yi kho na rtogs pa 'di//bdag ste bdag gi lag tu byin//chos la chos ni gnas par 
13a5(rgyud): 'gyur//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' ses dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gyi dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab dang ye

13a7(rgyud): kyi ral grir gyur te/de byung nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi thugs su zhugs te gcig tu 'dres par gyur nas/rdo rje ral gri'i dbyibs su gyur te bcom ldan 'das kyi phyag gnyis su gnas so//de nas rdo rje ral gri'i dbyibs de las 'jig rten gyi khams thams 
13b1(rgyud): cad kyi rdul shin tu phra mo dang mnyam pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rnams byung ste/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab dang ye shes la sogs pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzu 'phrul dang cho 'phrul rnams byas te/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni shin tu

13b3(rgyud): e ma'o sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//'jam dbyangs bdag ni yin par dgongs//gang phyir shes rab gzugs med kyang //dbyangs nyid du ni dmigs pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems/dpa' chen po 'jam dpal gyi sku de bcom ldan 'das kyi thugs 
13b4(rgyud): las byung ste/de bzhin gsheg pa thams cad kyi g.yas phyogs kyi zla ba'i dkyil 'khor la gnas nas yang bka' nod cing bzhugs par gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shes rab dang ye shes kyi dam tshig las 
13b5(rgyud): byung ba'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi nyon mongs pa thams cad gcad pa'i dam tshig dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus shing lus pa med pa rnams kyi sdug bsngal thams cad gcad pa dang /thams cad kyi bde ba dang 
13b6(rgyud): yid bde ba rnams myong bar byas nas de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi gsung gi rjes su 'brang ba'i shes rab dam pa yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i bar du/'bras bu de dag gi phyir/rdo rje ral gri de byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal de'i lag tu de bzhin du byin

14a1(rgyud): @#//'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i tshul//dgra rnams thams cad tshar gcod cing //sdig pa thams cad 'joms pa'i mchog/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems bskyed ma thag 
14a2(rgyud): tu chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i dam tshig las byung ba'i chos kyi byin gyis brlabs pa'i rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
14a3(rgyud): cad kyi snying po 'di rang gi thugs ka nas phyung ngo //badzra he tu/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thamasacad kyi thugs las byung ma thag pa 'di ni bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid de/rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po la sogs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyi dkyil 'khor du gyur

14a6(rgyud): sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin ni sems bskyed ma thag tu chosakyi 'khor lo bskor ba yin pa dang /shin tu brtan pa'i phyir gcig tu 'dres par gyur pas/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems bskyed ma thag tu chod kyi 'khor lo bskor ba zhes bya ba'i skur gyur te

23b1(rgyud): ru/de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs las de byung ma thag tu/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi dam tshig bsdams pa'i phyag na mang por gyur nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das rdo rje 'dzin pa de nyid do//de bzhin gshegs pa thams chod kyi dam tshig

26a1(rgyud): @#//'khor du//rdo rje rgya gram la sogs gzhag/'khor lo yi ni phra rnams su//rdo rje lha mo rnams kyang gzhag/dkyil 'khor phyi rim phra rnams su//sangs rgyas mchod pa rnams kyang gzhag/sgo rnams kyi ni nang du ni//sgo srung bzhi yang gzhag par

26a3(rgyud): so//maH/bdag la byin gyis brlab la sogs//ji lta bar ni bka' mod do//de ltar bgyis nas bdag ming brjod//de nas rdo rjes bsgrub bgyis//rdo rje slob dpon sems dpa' yi//rdo rje lcags kyu bcings nas ni//se gol mang po brdab gyis la//sangs rgyas 
26a4(rgyud): thams cad bsdu bar bgyi//de tshe sangs rgyas thams cad ni//rdo rje sems la sogs par bcas//dkyil 'khor thams cad gang ba rnams//dkyil 'khor rnams su 'dus par 'gyur//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' yi//phyag rgya chen po sgom byed cing //ming brgya rtsa brgyad dam 
26a5(rgyud): pa ni//cung zad rab tu brjod par bgyi//de nas 'dus pa dgyes pas na//de bzhin gshegs rnams brtan par 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' rab grub pa//grogs po nyid du nye bar gnas//de nas sgo rnams thams cad du//lcags kyu la sogs yas bgyis nas//
26a6(rgyud): las chen mchog gi phyag rgya yis//dam tshig rnams ni gzhag par bgyi//dam tshig mchog gi phyag rgya dang //sems dpa' rdo rje la sogs pas//de ltar sems dpa' chen po bsgrub//dzaHhU~M baM hoHzhes brjod cing //de nas sangs rgyas la sogs pa//sems dpa'

31b3(rgyud): dpa'i rdo rje bsgreng rdo rje//mdzub mo lcags kyu mdzub mo sbyar//legs so sbyin pa 'dra ba ni//rdo rje sems dpa' bzhi po yi//phyag rgya mang po'i dngos grub de'o//mthe bo gshibs la mdzub gnyis bcag/rtse sprad rin chen rdo rje yin//de nyid gung mo srin lag dang //mthe'u 
31b4(rgyud): chung rnams ni bsnol brkyang ba'o//de las srin lag mthe'u chung gnyis//mnyam par gshibs pa rgyal mtshan no//de 'di las ni bskor nas kyang //kha yi phyogs su bzhag pa'o//mthe bo gnyis ka mnyam par gshibs//mdzub mo gnyis ka bkug pa dang //gung

31b6(rgyud): bshad//de las mdzub mo gnyis gshibs la//snying gar brkyang ste bzhag pa'o//mdzub mo rtse mo gnyis bkug la//mthe'u chung gshibs pa mche ba'o//mthe'u chung gnyis par mthe bo gnyis//mdzub mo gnyis kyis btsir ba'o//snying gar mthe bo mnyam

60a6(rgyud): pa//de ni 'gugs pa'i dam pa'o//de dag gi snying po ni 'di yin no//oM badzra kro d+ha A ka rA ya hU~M phaT/oM badzra karma aM pu sha kro d+ha A ka rA ya hwA~M phaT/oM badzra kro d+ha daN+Da A gra A ka rA ya hU~M phaDa/oM badzra kro d+ha saM ga ts+tshA A ka rA ya mi te badzra sa ma ya ma nu sma ra hU~M phaT/de nas phyag rgya rnam 
60a7(rgyud): bzhi'i ye shes bslab bo//brkyang zhing bskum pa'i gnas pa ni//rdo rje dbang po'i tshul gyis ni//hU~M tsam gyis kyang skad cig gis//skye bo thams cad 'bebs par byed//rdo rje de bzhi legs par smras//hU~M zhes bya ba bzhi ldan pas//sangs rgyas kun gyi snying po yis//thams cad 
60b1(rgyud): kyang ni nges par 'gugs//rdo rje khro bor mnyam bzhag nas//rdo rje khro bo'i lha mchog gis//skye bo thams cad gson byed cig/rdo rje hUM gi rnal 'byor to//yid kyis lus la go bgo ba//bdag gam pha rol rnams la ste//go cha chen po'i sbyor bas ni//skye bo de dag thams cad bsrung //de nas 
60b2(rgyud): de dag gi snying po ni 'di dag yin no//oM badzra kro d+ha kA ya A be sha ya hUM phaT/oM badzra kro d+ha ma ma hA kro d+ha A na ya ho b+ha ga bA na badzra hUM phaT/oM badzra gro d+ha ti ShA ha na da ha ba tsa bi nA sha ya hUM ka re Sha pA ta ya badzra sa ma ya hUM phaT/oM ma no dr-i D+ha badzra ka ba tsa kro d+ha rak+Sha hUM phaT/de nas sems can thams 
60b3(rgyud): cad rgyas btab pa'i phyag rgya'i ye shes dang /rdo rje khro bor mnyam bzhag ni//badzra hUM zhes bzlas pa yis//gang dang gang la kun zhugs pa//phyag rgyas btab par de gyur to//sgra chen du ni brjod par bya//Ta ki hUM phaT/khro gnas lan cig gang la smras//rdo rje 'bar bas 
60b4(rgyud): de mngon bkug/rdo rje khro bo'i ting 'dzin cing //rdo rje'i lta bas bltas pa yis//sems can gang dang gang yin pa//de dang de ni 'chi bar 'gyur//gsod snyam yid la bsams nas su//snying gar rdo rje sgom par byed//hUM zhes bya ba yid kyis su//rang gi rgyas ni 'debs 
60b5(rgyud): par dgongs//sems can gang la gang gis su//yid kyi rgyas ni 'debs pa 'di//phyag rgya 'dis ni shin tu yang //las rnams thams cad bsgrub par bya//de nas de dag gi snying po ni 'di dag go//Tak+ki a/Tak+ki pho/Tak+ki hUM/Tak+ki hoM/phyag rgya re res kyang las bzhi rnams la phyag rgya'i 
60b6(rgyud): snying po bzhis sbyar ro//de nas gsang ba khro bo'i phyag rgya'i ye shes bslab bo//yan lag kun gyis 'khyud nas ni//hUM zhes yul la sbyar byang //dbang po gnyis ni sprad byas nas//de yi srog ni ma rung 'gyur//hUM zhes bya bar ldan pa yis//mchu sprad mos ni mchu bzung nas//dbang po 
60b7(rgyud): gnyis ni rab sprad pa//gang yin de ni mngon sum 'gyur//hUM zhes sbyor ba gang yin pa//de ni bde ba myong bar 'gyur//dbang po gnyis ni sprang gyur na//gang yin de ni sdug bsngal 'thob//hUM zhes byang rab sbyor zhing //dbang po gnyis ni spyad nas kyang //yan lag kun gyis 
61a1(rgyud): @#//btsir gyur na//de yi lus ni bsrungs bar 'gyur//de nas de dag gi snying po ni 'di dag go//hUM a/hUM dza/hUM ho/hUM haM/hUM he/de nas 'jig rten gsum las rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i dam tshig de nyid kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes bslab bo//gang gis sems can phan pa'i phyir//
61a2(rgyud): rdo rje dbyings kyi tshul gyis ni//sangs rgyas rjes su dran byas na//de ni sangs rgyas 'thob par 'gyur//dbang phyug dang ni yum yang //sa la bris te de ltar gnan//de ltar bris pa'i go rims bzhin//sems dpa'i phyag rgya bcing bar bya//de ni bcings pa tsam gyis kyang //de ni 'jig rten 
61a3(rgyud): gsum rgyal ba//rdo rje hUM dang 'dra ba yi//dngos grub rdzu 'phrul myur du 'grub//ji ltar ri bong bris pa bzhin//'bar bas bskor ba'i dbus bzhugs pa//sku gsung thugs ni rdo rje yis//sems dpa'i phyag rgya pa bcing bar bya//de nas de rnams las bshad de//rdo rje las ni bla na med//
61a4(rgyud): sangs rgyas rjes su dran grub pas//myur du sangs rgyas nyid 'thob bo//'jig rten gsum las rgyal bcings na//'jig rten gsum las rnam rgyal 'gyur//rdo rje che dang kun du 'gro//rdo rje hUM zhes bya ba 'dra//rdo rje dbang gis rgyal por 'gyur//chos bzang ldan pas 'jig rten 
61a5(rgyud): dbang //las kyi rdo rje khro bo che//rdo rje las ni byed par 'gyur//sems dpa' khro bor rab tu brtan//khro bo'i lcags kyus yang dag 'gugs//khro bo chags pas chags par byed//khro bo legs pas dga' ba ster//khro gnyer gyis kun ma rungs byed//khro bo nyi mas gzi brjid 
61a6(rgyud): mchog/dpal gyi khro bo don rnams sdud//ha ha dgod pas bdud byed do//chos kyi khro bos chos rnams bdud//rdo rje ral gris gcod pa ste//rgyu'i khro bo sdug bsngal 'phrog/khro bo smra bas tshig 'phrog go//las kyi khro bo las rab ste//srung ba yis ni srung bar byed//khro bo gnod 
61a7(rgyud): sbyin dgra yang za//khro bo'i khu tshur dngos grub ster//de nas rdo rje dam tshig gi phyag rgya bcing ba bshad do//rdo rje gnyis su gyur pa ste//dam tshig mchog tu grags pa yin//de dag bcing ba bshad par bya//khro bo'i bcing ba bla na med//lag ngar rdo rjer byas nas ni//mthe'u chung lcags 
61b1(rgyud): gyur pa cing phar bya//mdzub mo gnyis kyis bsdigs pa ni//'jig rten gsum las rgyal zhes bya//de bzhin mdzub mo ngos gyur te//nor bu 'dra bar rab tu dgug/phung po nam mkha' bsgreng ba dang bya//phung mo mdzub mo gnyis kas bsdigs//mdzub mo gnyis ka rdo rjer bya//g.yas pa lcags kyur dgug 
61b2(rgyud): par bya//de nyid hUM zhes bya bar mdzad//legs pa sbyin par de bzhin te//mdzub gnyis dpral ba'i gnyer mar bzhag/snying gar mdzub mos nyi mar bskor//lag pa brkyang ste spyi bor bzhag/mdzub mo kha nas dgod ltar bya//mdzub mo'i len mo sgyur ba ste//g.yas pas ral gri bcing pa 
61b3(rgyud): pa'o//gung mdzub mnyam pa sgreng 'khor lo yi//kha yi nang nas dbyung ba dang //mdzub mo gung mo rdo rjer bya//mdzub mo gnyis ni mche ba che//mdzub mo bkug pa rdo rje rab cing sa//rdo rje lA s+ye la sogs pa//sems ma'i phyag rgyas de nyid hUM//zhes dbye chos kyi phyag rgya de//hUM 
61b4(rgyud): zhes bya bar lhan cig go//de nas rdo rje'i dam tshig gi chos kyi phyag rgya rnams bshad do//hUM ni sangs rgyas rdo rje yi//hUM ni rdo rje snying po yi//hrI ni rdo rje sde yi pho//a ni rdo rje thams cad kyi//hUM he/traM/taM/hi hi de ha/d+hi ka d+hi hUM/grA/kri paM/dr-i ya/rdo rje khro bo mang po yi//chos kyi 
61b5(rgyud): phyag rgya rab 'grub pa'o//de nas rdo rje dam tshig gi las kyi phyag rgya rnams bshad de//rdo rje'i rigs ni chen po yi//las kyi phyag rgya mdor bsdus na//rdo rje 'gying ba sogs tshul gyis//khro bo'i khu tshur gnyis su bshad//gang dang gang gi phyag rgya rnams//las kyi 
61b6(rgyud): phyogs ni gang du bya//de dang der ni bsgyur nas kyang //phyag rgya de ni sbyar bar bya//phyag rgya thams cad kyi cho ga'o//de nas 'dir 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i spyi'i phyag rgya bcing ba bshad do//'jig rten gsum rgyal phyag rgya ni//
61b7(rgyud): rdo rje mdzub mo dam tshig gi//rdo rje hUM zhes sngags kyi ste//dngos grub thams cad phun gyis 'thob//mthe'u chung gnyi ga lcags kyur bcing //rdo rje gnyi ga'i bla 'og tu//dam tshig lcags kyu'i phyag rgya de//sa/tshig gis ni thams cad 'gugs//
62a1(rgyud): @#//rig pa dam pa thams cad kyi//'jig rten gsum las rgyal zhes bshad//thams cad kyang ni gnod pa ste//de bzhin las rnams thams cad byed//rdo rje mdzub sor gnyis legs par//rab mnyam bzhag pas mnyam nas ni//shi ba thams cad slong ba ste//rdo rje slong ba zhes bya'o//
62a2(rgyud): rdo rje gnyis ka legs par ni//rdo rje bcing bas bcings nas su//bzlog cig tsug tu bzhag pa ni//tshe dang nad med 'phel ba'o//rdo rje bcing ba dam byas te//mthe bo bsnyams nas nang du bzhug/mdzub mo dam por bkug nas su//bcug pa rkang par 'gugs pa'o//'jig rten 
62a3(rgyud): gsum rgyal bcings nas su//gang gi gzugs la rkang g.yon pas//legs par mnan pa'i sems can de//zla ba phyed kyis grub par 'gyur//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyis bcings te//gcig la gcig ni brdabs na ni//sems can gang gi lus la ni//babs na de yang rdob par byed//
62a4(rgyud): rdo rje phyag rgya gnyis bcing ste//bdag gam pha rol go char bya//mdzub mo gnyis kyis bcings byas na//des pa nyid du srung bar 'gyur//rdo rje mthil du bcings nas kyang //yid kyis khros nas dag pa bya ba//mthe bo rdo rje dam po ste//rdo rje rigs chen grub par 'gyur//
62a5(rgyud): sems dpa' rdo rje srar bya ste//mthe bo gnyis kas gung mo mnan//mthe'u chung rdo rje rtse mo ni//rnon por dam tshig 'dzin pa'o//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyis pa bcings te//mdzub mo gnyis bkug bcings nas su//gcu zhing phyir gcu byas pa ni//lha dgug pa yi dam pa yin//
62a6(rgyud): rdo rje bcing ba dam byas te//mdzub mo gnyis pa ku ga rab tu mnan//mthe bo gnyis ka sbyar ba yis//dbus su bcing ba bar snang gi'o//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyi ga las//mthe'u chung gnyi ga bcings nas su//rgyab sbyor mdzub mo gnyis sbrel te//bsgyur ba mkha' la spyod pa'i 'o//
62a7(rgyud): rdo rje bcings pa bsdams nas su//rdo rje srung bas bcing bar bya//de ni sa pa'i dam tshig ste//thams cad byed pas 'da' bar dka'//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyis bcings te//g.yon pa'i rdo rje mdzub mo la//mthe'u chung 'khyud cing rtag tu gtogs//de ni sa 'og 'gugs pa'o//
62b1(rgyud): rdo rje bcings pas sbrel ba ni//mdzub mo gnyis ka dam po ste'o//gung mo mthe bo rdo rjer ni//gdug pa'i phyag rgya 'joms pa'o//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyis bcings te//mnyam bzhag ste ni snying gar bzhag/khro bo khu tshur gyis btsir ba//phyi rol dkyil 'khor 'jig pa'o//g.yon pa'i 
62b2(rgyud): rdo rje mdzub mo la//g.yas pa'i mdzub mo bkug pas bzung //rab tu khros nas drag gtogs na//ri rab kyang ni lhung bar byed//g.yon pa'i rdo rje mdzub mo la//g.ya sa pa'i mdzub mo lcags kyus bzung //rab tu khros shing bkug pa yis//gdon rnams kun la 
62b3(rgyud): dbang du byed//rdo rje bcing ba byas nas su//lag ngar snying gar shin tu bsdams//rdo rje mdzub mos nyid ma chan khung //brud pa yis ni snying sbyin no//mdzub mo gnyis kyi rtse mo yis//mthe bong gnyis kyi drung du ni//yid kyis khros nas btsir ba bya//rims 
62b4(rgyud): la sogs dgug dam pa'o//rdo rje thal mo dam sbyar te//mdzub mo gnyi ga bkug nas su//mthe bor rab tu mnyam sbyar bas//mnan pa de ni sdig 'phrog pa'o//rdo rje'i phyag rgya gnyis nang du//mdzub mo gnyis ni sbas nas kyang //legs par gyen du 'phangs pa 
62b5(rgyud): ni//lhung ba slong ba'i dam pa'o//rdo rje bcing ba dam byas te//gung mo gnyis ka ngos sbyar te//tha ma bzhi yi ngos sprad pa//skad cig tu ni sdig 'dral lo//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor chen po bsgrub pa'i phyag rgya bcing ba bshad do//rdo 
62b6(rgyud): rje'i phyag rgyas bzung nas ni//dkyil 'khor dang por srad bu bre//srad bu bres la phyi nas ni//srad bu ji bzhin bre ba bshad//rdo rje phyag rgya gnyi ga yis//mdzub mo'i rtse mor rab sbyar ba//byas nas mtshon rnams thams cad la//'bar ba'i grwa pas dgug par bya//rdo rje'i mdzub 
62b7(rgyud): mos legs par ni//sbyar la bkan te bsdams nas su//rab tu khros nas dbye ba ni//sgo dbye ba yi mchog yin no//de nas rdo rje rigs rnams kyi phyag rgya thams cad bsgrub pa bshad do//g.yon brkyang ba yi tshul byas te//khro bo yi ni skad 'byin cing //
63a1(rgyud): @#//khro bo'i blta bas bltas na ni//las rnams thams cad sgrub par byed//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje dam tshig gi rtog pa chen po'i rgyal po las 'jig rten gsum las rnam par rgyal ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po'i cho ga rgyas pa rdzogs s+ho////
63b1(rgyud): rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor der//dbus su mdud rtse mchur bris la//rdo rje rab tu gzhag bya zhing //'od 'bar dbus su bri bar bya//de yi go rims ji bzhin te//rdo rje rdo rje lcags kyu dang //mda' dang dga' bde bzhin no//rdo rje shugs kyis brgal nas ni//dkyil 'khor dam pa gnyis pa 
63b2(rgyud): yang //'khor lo'i dbus na gnas pa yi//rdo rje rin chen bri bar bya//tho gnyer dbus su rdo rje bri//de bzhin rdo rje rgyal mtshan//de bzhin so yi phreng ba de'i//dngos su rdo rje bri bar bya//rdo rje shugs kyis brga la nas ni//dkyil 'khor ram pa gsum pa yang //pad+ma'i 
63b3(rgyud): dbus su gnas pa yi//rdo rje pad+ma lha rdzas bri//'bar ba'i dbus su pad+ma bri//ral gri 'khor lo de bzhin no//rdo rje'i lce yang tshul bzhin du//rtag tu de yi phyogs su gzhag/rdo rje shugs kyis brgalanas ni//dkyil 'khor bzhi pa dam par yang //rdo 
63b4(rgyud): rje bsnyal la rdo rje bri//rdo rje'i 'od ni chen pos bskor//de yi phyogs ni thams cad du//'bar ba kun gyis dkrigs pa'i 'od//rdo rje thams cad go cha mchog/rdo rje mche ba khu tshur mchog/gla dang phyi yi gnas rnams la//tshul bzhin du ni bri bar bya//
63b5(rgyud): de nas rab tu bshad bya ba//phyag rgya rig pa mdor bsdu na//dz+hiH/oM badzra kro d+he sa ma ye dz+hiH/oM badzra krod+he ro Sha aM ku sha A na ya sarba na dz+hiH/oM badzra ro She kA ma/badzri Ni ba sham+me A na ya hi dz+hiH/oM badzra duSh+Ta kro d+he to Sha ya sarba na dz+hiH/rdo rje dz+hiHzhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor ro//
63b6(rgyud): dziH/oM badzra g+h+raM shu TI kro d+he ha ra sarba ar+tha na dziH/oM badzra dz+wa la mA lA pra be sha ma hA kro d+hi ag+ni dz+wA la ya sarba na ra bi ro She dziH/oM badzra d+ha dza a gre ke yU ra ma hA kro d+he de hi me sarba ar+tha na dziH/o badzra yaT+Ta ha sa ni hA sa hA sa a T+TA T+Te hA se ne mA ra ya dziH/rdo rje dziHzhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor
63b7(rgyud): diH/oM badzra shu d+ha kro d+he ha na ha na mA ra ya duSh+ka na diH/oM badzra tI k+Sha kro d+hi ts+tshin+da/badzra ko She na/sarba ni di n+na/oM badzra he ru ma hA kro d+he pra be sha tsakra pra be sha ya sa na diH/oM badzra dzi dam hA kro d+he ba she ba tsa muHdeH/rdo rje diHzhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor

64b1(rgyud): mnyes nas dgos pa thams cad ni//'dod bzhin skad cig nyid kyis 'grub//de la gar gyi phyag rgya'i snying po ni 'di rnams yin no//sid+d+ha badzra/A na ya badzra/rA ga ya badzra/sA d+hu badzra/de'i 'og tu lan gyi gar gyi mchod pa bya ste/de bzhin gar gyi g.yon pa ni//lu tshur g.yas

64b7(rgyud): bya//rdo rje mche ba bcings nas ni//rdo rje'i bcing bas mnan par bya//mchod pa 'di ni cho ga yis//las rnams kun kyang nus par 'gyur//mchod pa rnam bzhi byas nas ni//cho ga bzhin du phyag rgya bkrol//de la de dag gi snying 
65a1(rgyud): @#//po ni 'di rnams yin no//ts+r-i t+ya badzra/rak+Sha badzra/khA da badzra/khA da badzra/ban+da badzra/de nas khro bo'i gsang ba'i phyag rgya'i ye shes bslab bo//rdo rje gsang ba'i lag gnyis kyis//khros pa'i yid kyis drag btags bya//skye bo gang gi ming smos pa//de yi snying ni 'gas par 'gyur//
65a2(rgyud): ma mchu so yis bzung nas su//gang gi ming smos gzir ba ni//de yi mgo yang myur 'gas te//gang gi bka' las 'das pa'i 'o//khro bo khro chen lta ba yi//mig kyang 'dzums par byed pa ni//gang gi ming ni thos pa lhas pa//de yi mig ni rdol bar 'gyur//rdo rje khro bor mnyam 
65a3(rgyud): bzhag la//bdag rang gi yang snying gar yang //rdo rje bcings pa bcing na ni//de yi sems ni 'jig par byed//de la de dag gi snying po rnams ni 'di yin no//hU~M badzra mI ka badzra thaH/hU~M badzra ni trA thaH/hU~M badzra ma ni thaH/de nas rdo rje chen po'i rigs kyi gsang ba'i

65a5(rgyud): brkyang //de bzhin bkan pa pad+ma ni//de bzhin du yang bsgyur nas ni//g.yon pa'i rdo rjer gzhag par bya//rdo rje mthil du bcings byas te//khros pa yis ni dga' bya zhing //mthe bo rdo rje dam pas so//khro bo ti rin+ti rir bshad//mdzub mo lcags kyu dgug pa

65a7(rgyud): bzhin//gnyis kar la gri chang bar 'dzin//mdzub gnyis 'khor lo rab cings pa'o//mdzub mo bsnyams te kha nas dbyung //mdzub mo legs par brkyang ba dang //mdzub mos mgul du bcing bar bya//de gnyis kha 'gram mche bar bzhag/khu tshur dam du bcings

70b4(rgyud): pa'i chang bas gzung bar bya//bsgyur nas dpral ba nyid du ni//mdzub mo'i ngos ni gzhag par bya//de bzhin gar yang bkrel nas ni//mnyam par pad+ma kha 'bus sbyar//mdzub mo snying gar bzhag nas ni//bsam pa g.yos pas 'dud par bya'o//rdo rje khro ba'i mdzub bsnyams

106b1(rgyud): rgya chen po bcings nas kyang //de nyid chen po tshig re rer//brjod nas kun dang ldan par yang //thams cad bdag ni yin bsgoms na//ci ste 'dod na bdag nyid des//de bya gzi can dge ba yi//go 'phang tshe 'di nyid la 'thob//mi 'dod dngos grub gong ma bzhin//de nas

107a1(rgyud): @#//phyag rgya chen po'i dngos grub kyi rgyud bshad de/'dod chags ngo bo nyid kyis dag/mu stegs can gyis mi gyur la//de la chags pa'i chos 'di ni//theg pa che la 'grub par 'gyur//de nas dam tshig gi phyag rgya dam pa'i dngos grub ni 'di yin te/ting 'dzin

107a3(rgyud): 'dod chags pha rol phyin rnyed de//skad cig gis ni gzi can 'thob//de nas las kyi phyag rgya dam pa'i dngo grub kyi rgyud bshad de/bdag gi lta dang reg pa dang //mnyan pa dang ni dran pa'ang rung //sems can rnams ni thams cad kyi//sdug bsngal kun gyi mthar phyin 
107a4(rgyud): 'gyur//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes nor bu'i rigs kyi dam pa'i dngos grub kyi rgyud 'di gsungs so//de la phyag rgya chen po dam pa'i dngos grub kyi rgyu 'di yin te/rdo rje snying po ji bzhin du//sangs rgyas kun dbang bdag gyur ces//bsgom 
107a5(rgyud): par bya ba rnams bsgoms nas//skad cig gis ni gzi tsan 'thob//de nas dam tshig gi phyag rgya dam pa'i dngos grub kyi brgyud bshad de/nam mkha'i snying po 'dra bar ni//sems can kun gyi re ba kun//yongs su rdzogs par byed gyur cing //skad cig gis ni gzi tan 'thob//de nas chos kyi 
107a6(rgyud): phyag rgya dam pa'i dngos grubakyi rgyud bshad de/bdag nyid kyang ni spangs nas su//nor byin rab tu dga' byed cing //chos byung phyag rgya smra na ni//gzi can tshe 'di nyid la 'thob//de nas las kyi phyag rgya dam pa'i dngos sgrub kyi rgyud bshad de//dbul po rnams la phan pa'i phyir//
107a7(rgyud): nor bsgrub pa la brtson ldan na//gzi can 'gyur ro ci ste yang //mi 'dod dngos grub gong ma bzhin//rigs thams cad kyi phyag rgya rnams kyi sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dam pa rnams kyi dngos grub thob pa'i cho ga rgyas pa'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems

107b4(rgyud): ni 'di yin te/rang bzhin dag par shes nas kyang //de yi dgos pa de byed de//pad+ma'i rigs ni chen po yi//dam tshig 'da' bar dka' ba'o//de nas nor bu'i rigs kyi dam tshig gi dngaus grub kyi rgyud ni 'di yinate/nyung ngam yang na mang yang rung //ci tsam nus pa nyid du 
107b5(rgyud): ni//sbyin pas nyi ma stongs par ni//bya min dam tshig 'di yin no//rigs thams cad kyi dam tshig gi dngos grub kyi cho ga rgyas pa'i rgyud do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rigs kyi chos kyi phyag rgya'i ye shes kyi dngos grub 
107b6(rgyud): kyi rgyud 'di gsungs so//de la de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi chos kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud ni 'di yin te/sangs rgyas chos zhes grags so zhes//brjod na chos nyid yi ge ni//sangs rgyas chos kyi phyag rgya che//ye shes mchog gi tshul yin no//de la de bzhin gshegasapa'i rigs kyi chos

111a1(rgyud): @#//kyis gsungs so//de la 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi dam tshig gi de kho na nyid kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud do//su ra ta stAM zhes brjod na//de nyid bskul ba'i bdag nyid che//ma bsgrubs par yang phyag rgya kun//nyid kyis bcings na 'grub par 'gyur//zhes bcom ldan 'das dam tshig chen po'i de kho na nyid kyis gsungs so//de la 'ad ni rdo rje'i rigs kyi de kho na nyid kyi dngos gru ba kyi rgyud do//hU~M zhes bya ba gcig tsam gyis//mdor na phyag rgya thams cad ni//bdag gam yang na gzhan yang rung //nyid bcings 'ching du bcug kyang rung //de la 'di ni pad+ma'i rigs kyi dam tshig gi dekho na nyid kyi dngos grub gyi rgyud do//sarba shud b+hA zhes brjod na//bdag gam yang na gzhad dag gis//bud med chags la sogs ldan na//thams cad du ni grol mi 'gyur//de la 'di ni nor bu'i rigs kyi dam tshig gi da kho na nyid kyi dngos grub kyang rgyud do//oM zhes bya bas mdor bsdu na//phyag rgya thams cad 'grub 'gyur te//'jig rten kun gyi mchog nyid kyi//rang 'byung nyid la mchod par 'gyur//rigs thams cad kyi dam tshig gi de kho na nyid kyi dngos grub kyi cho ga rgyas pa'i rgyud do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dngos grub kyi phyag rgya'i rgyud 'di gsungs so//de la 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i dngos grub kyi rgyud 'di yin te/de bzhin gshegs pa jar byed cing //sangs rgyas phyag rgya bcings nas ni//sangs rgyas byang chub brtan par yang //bsgrubs na myur du 'grub par 'gyur//de la 'di ni de bzhinagashegs pa'i rigs kyi dngos grub kyi rgyud do//rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya che//bcings nas de yi mdun du yang //rdo rje sems dpa' bsgoms na ni//shin tu myur bar dngos grub 'gyur//de la 'di ni rdo rje'i rigs kyi dngos grub kyi brgyud do//rdo rje sems pa'i ting 'dzin gyis//dam tshig gzhi ni mchog bcings na//rdo rje sems dpa' sgoms na ni//nyi ma du ni dngos grub 'gyur//de la'ang ni pad+ma'i rigs kyi dngos grub kyis rgyud do//'jig rten dbang

116a5(rgyud): gsungs so//de la 'di ni nor bu'i rigs kyi bsrung ba'i nga dos grub kyi rgyud de/sangs rgyas sems can gtong byed par//ci nus par ni sbyor byed cing //nyi ma stong par mi bya bar//bsrung ba 'di ni rmad? kyi mchog/ces bcom ldan 'das rdo rje bsrung bas gsungs so//rigs

116a7(rgyud): cad brtag pa thams cad kyi dbang phyug tu gyur nas rtogs pa rab kyi ste/myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i slad du'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' che po phyag na rdo rje la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/phyag na 
116b1(rgyud): rdo rje la legs par bshad pas nged kyis blangs shing byin gyis brlabs kyis legs so legs so//de nas yang de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad gcig tu 'dus nas byang chub sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rje la legs so zhes bya ba byin te/rdo rje theg pa bla na med//de bzhin gshegs

116b4(rgyud): cad kyi dngos grub dam pa'i gsang ba'i rgyud de/sangs rgyas byang chub ting nge 'dzin//rab mnyam bzhag pas bsgoms na ni//byang chub bde bas thob par 'gyur//bsams nas de ni rtogs par bya//zhes bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad kyis gsungs so//
116b5(rgyud): de la 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa tha mas cad kyi rigs dam pa'i gsang ba'i dngos grub kyi brgyud de/sems dpa' rdo rje snying gar bcings//nga yi chung ma khyod sdug pa//brtan par gyur cig ces smras nas//phyag rgya thams cad bsgrub bya ste//cho ga 'di yi tshul gyis ni//rang gi

116b7(rgyud): par ma gyur na//hU~M gi phyag rgya sa bsad na ni//skrag ste dngos grub sbyin par byed//ces bcom ldan 'das de bzhIn gshegs pa thams cad kyis hU~M zhes bya bas gsungs so//de la 'dir ni pad+ma'i rigs kyi dngos grub dam pa'i gsang ba'i rgyud de/ting 'dzin phyag rgya bcings

118a1(rgyud): @#//thams cad kyi las kyi dngos grub gsang ba'i rgyud 'di gsungs so//de la 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i las kyi dngos grub gsang pa'i rgyud de/rdo rje sems dpa'i ting 'dzin gyis//sangs rgyas rjes su dran bsgoms la//bud med lugs kyi bu gar blugs//

121b1(rgyud): ces bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor los gsungs so//rigs thams cad bskyed pa'i gsang ba'i dngos grub kyi ma brgyas pa'i rgyud do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po phyag na rdo rjes de bzhin gshegs pa tham sa cad

121b4(rgyud): gzung ba'i tshul gyis lcags kyu yin//phra mo rtogs phyir mda' yin la//mgu bar gyur phyir legs zhes bya//smras pa'i phyir yang rin chen gsungs//gzi brjid tshul gyis nyi ma ste//rgyal mtshan legs par spang bar gsungs//'dzum phyir bzhad par grags

122a5(rgyud): par 'gyur//zhes bcom ldang 'das rdo rje dam tshig gis gsungs so//de la 'di ni chos kyi rigs kyi phyag rgya bcing ba skye bar 'gyur ba'i dngos grub gsang ba'i rgyud de/mdor na rgyal bas rdo rje bka'//'da' bar bya ba ma yin te//'di ni bcings par shes nas kyang //des

154a7(rgyud): dgu rnams//'jig rten gsum po zhes pa ste//rang byung nga nyid kho na yin//'dod pa zhes bya chos chen te//dag pa btsal bar bya ba'i phyir//gzung 'dzin chos rnams zhes bya ba'i//de bzhin nyid las so sor rtog/chos kyi dbyings dang dbyer med phyir//nga ni 
154b1(rgyud): chos de dag par byed//de bas nga ni rgyal po che//'jig rten gsum gyi bdag por bstan//ji ltar 'dod pa de bzhin gzugs//de bzhin gzugs med rab tu bsgrags//stong pa nyid dang dbyer med pa'i//nga ni snying po'i bdag por bshad//khams gsum pa yi don ci zhig/'dod 
154b2(rgyud): khams de bzhin gzugs khams dang //gzugs med pa ni nyid dag dang //khams gsum bzang po 'di dag ni//rang byung ba nyid kho na yin//'di ni khams dang rgyu dang don//khams gsum gyi ni rgyu sems pa//stong dang mtshan ma med bshad cing //smon pa med pa nga nyid 
154b3(rgyud): bstan//'khor ba'i don ni ci zhig yin//byis pa rnams ni kun du 'byung //de bzhin yang dang yang du 'khor//'khor ba dag pa'i bdag nyid de//rang byung nga nyid kho na yin//gang la 'khor ba yod pa yi//chos ni rdul tsam yod ma yin//yang dag ma yin kun 
154b4(rgyud): brtags phyir//nga ni thog mtha' med pa yin//med grang de ni stong pa ste//stong pa nyid gang nga nyid do//de bas 'khor ba de nyid ni//rdo rje 'chang rgyal nga nyid bsgrags//mya ngan 'das pa ci yin brjod//gang zhig de bzhin nyid rtag stong //bden bzhi rang bzhin 
154b5(rgyud): bzang po nyid//mtshan nyid dang ni dngos po gzugs//mtshan ma nyid kyang rnam par spangs//tshig dang sgra yis gzung bya yin//rnam par rtog pa yod ma yin//de bas mya ngan 'das sgra ru//nga nyid kho na rab shes bya//de yi rtag pa'i don ci zhig/rtag nyid rtag la 'byung gnas 
154b6(rgyud): te//rnam par rtog pa dang bral gang //rtag pa de nyid kho na ni//mya ngan 'das pa'i sgra ru bsgrags//'jig pa yang ni ci yin brjod//gang zhig/'khor bar mi 'jug cing //thams cad mi rtag pa nyid dang //sdug bsngal 'byung ba 'gog byed pa'i//'jig pa mya ngan 'das 
154b7(rgyud): su yin//dag pa yang ni ci yin brjod //yod pa'ang ma yin med pa'ang min//dngos po'ang ma yin dngos med min//brjod bya rjod byed ma yin min//dag pa mya ngan 'das nyid yin//theg pa chen po ci yin brjod//sangs rgyas thams cad gshegs 
155a1(rgyud): @#//gyur cing //theg pa kun las nges rgyal ba//yang dag gyur pa'i sems can ni//theg pa chen po zhes su bstan//mthu dang las rnams 'jug pa dang //tshogs rnams rnal 'byor ngo mtshar rnams//rgya che zhes pa theg chen te//sangs rgyas kun gyi ngo mtshar mchog/
155a2(rgyud): nam mkha'i don ni ci zhig yin//sa nyid kyang ni ci yin brjod//'byung ba med cing rdzas kyang med//skye ba med cing 'chi ba med//dus gsum dngos po dang bral mchog/nam mkha' zhes pa'i sgra ru bstan//yang dang yang du skye ba dang //de bzhin nyon mongs kun bdag nyid//grangs 
155a3(rgyud): med dpag med dang bral ba//de ni sa nyid brjod par bya//'byung ba chen po gang las byung //'gog pa ci yin gang gis byed//ma rig nyon mongs la sogs las//'byung ba chen po yang dag 'byung //sa bon kun ldan kun gzhi ni//phyi dang nang gi bdag nyid 'dod//

155a6(rgyud): btab par 'gyur//ci phyir sngags su brjod par bya//gsang sngags rnams ni 'bod byed cing //gdug pa thams cad skrod par byed//nyon mongs sngags rnams 'joms byed cing //sangs rgyas chos ni spyan 'dren yin//ci phyir zhe na rig sngags brjod//'dod 
155a7(rgyud): chags mun pa rnams 'dul zhing //nyon mongs rnams kyang rnam par 'dul//ma rig pa ni zlog byed pa//nga ni rig sngags legs par brjod//gzungs sngags rnams ni ci yin brjod//chos rnams kyi ni rten byed cing //srid pa'i phyogs ni rab 'joms byed//
155b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas chos rnams thob byed cing //dge ba la rten sbyin pa'ang yin//phyag rgya yi ni mtshan nyid brjod//sangs rgyas kun dang brtul zhugs ldan//phyi ma thub sems dpa' rnams dang ni//rdo rje 'chang ba thams cad dag/sbyong lam phyag rgya mtshon par byed//sngags rnams kyi ni mtshan nyid 
155b2(rgyud): kyang //sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs yin te//chos kyi snying po sgrub byed cing //chos kyi dbyings dang yang dag ldan//sngags kyi mtshan nyid yin par brjod//rig sngags kyi ni mtshan nyid gsungs//'khor bar myong ba'i sdug bsngal dang //'dod chags la sogs nyes pa'i 
155b3(rgyud): tshogs//rigs dang bcas pa ni byed pa'i//rig sngags kyi ni mtshan/nyid brjod//de bzhin kho nar gzungs sngags kyi//sangs rgyas chos rnams 'dzin byed cing //chos rnams 'dzin par byed pa dang //dge ba zhes bya 'dzin byed pa//gzungs sngags kyi ni mtshan nyid brjod//
155b4(rgyud): rtsa ba'i phyag rgya'i gnas ni gang //ji ltar bcing bya thub chen po//phyag rgya dag ni 'ching ba na//ji ltar rdo rje can kun la nyes//dang po kho na 'bras bu bya zhing //de bzhin phyogs dag bcing ba dang //log 'dren rnams kyang bcing bya ste//gos dag gtsang ma bgo bya 
155b5(rgyud): zhing //grong dang yul 'khor dag dang ni//grong khyer dang ni grong rdal rnams//de yi dbus su phyag rgya dag/nam yang bcing bar mi bya ste//lha khang yang na dgon pa'am//ri bo yi ni phug dag gam//rnam pa sna tshogs kyis dben zhing //'du 'dzi med pa'i 
155b6(rgyud): gnas gtsang ma//de bzhin skyil krung bca' bar bya//bkra shis skyil krung nyid bya ste//ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' yis//byang chub snying por bcings pa bzhin//der ni mig dag btsum bya zhing //so so ni gzhag par bya//mchu gnyis dag 
155b7(rgyud): kyang mnyam bya zhing //dran pa rtse gcig byas nas kyang //sbyor med sbyor ba'i sngags bzla zhing //phyag rgya chen po bcing bar bya//phyag rgya'i rnam pa de lta bu'i//bcings pas rdo rje can kun mnyes//rig sngags ji ltar bzla bar bya//dbang phyug chen po gsang bas 
156a1(rgyud): gsungs//rig sngags dag ni zlos pa na//ji ltar yid kyis rdo rje bzlas//skyil krung ji lta ba bzhin bya//dran dang yid ni ma g.yengs pas//rig sngags dag ni bzla bya zhing //sku nyid kyang ni bsam par bya//mthu yis ma yin gcig kyang min//rgan

156a3(rgyud): nor ster dpal dang g.yang //sku la bzang rnam pa dang ldan zhing //dam bca' ba dang bkra shis don//nor bu 'dzin pa'i snying po yin//yi ge hU~M ni ci yin brjod//sdug bsngal 'chos shing 'joms par byed//srid pa'i zhags pa gcod byed cing //gdug pa 
156a4(rgyud): sngangs shing gsod byed pa'i//hU~M ni mchog tu dran par bya//yi ge phaT ni ci yin brjod//gdug dang sdug bsngal 'joms byed cing //bde ba dag ni 'gugs par byed//phaT ni 'joms byed sgra yin te//grogs dang bcas pa 'joms par byed//swA hA zhes pa ci yin brjod//
156a5(rgyud): sdig pa thams cad zhi byed cing //dge ba'i tshogs ni 'phel bar byed//nyes par byas pa 'phrog byed cing //sdug bsngal thams cad 'joms pa'i mchog/mchod pa ci phyir brjod par bya//mchod par byed pas mchod pa ste//gang du gsad bya mi 'jigs pa'i//de yi 
156a6(rgyud): mchod pa rnam mang 'gyur//phyi rol gyi ni mchod pa min//'di ni sangs rgyas kun gyis mchod//de bzhin du ni thar pa'i mchog/gsang ba'i mchod pa de rnams ni//'jigs med rgyal sras rnams kyi ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin dang //nyid kyi sras kyis mtshungs 
156a7(rgyud): med pa'i//cho ga bsgrub pa'i gsang ba ni//rtag tu rig pa 'dzin pa'i mchog/de bzhin dam tshig shes nyid kyang //thugs pa gang dang gang yin pa//de ni dam tshig shes pa yin//dam tshig goms par byed pa yang //dam tshig shes pa yin par 
156b1(rgyud): brjod//bskal pa grangs med 'das pa yi//byang chub sems dang mtshungs par 'gyur//gsang ba'i dam tshig bzhugs pa//de yi dam tshig shes par brjod//ci yi phyir na dkyil 'khor yin//dam pa'i chos kyi bcud snying po//'byung bas de ni dkyil 'khor brjod//
156b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po gsang ba che//chos chen shin tu bstan pa'o//dkyil 'khor tshig ji lta bu//cho ga chos dang rnal 'byor te//gsang ba'i thar pa yang dag 'byung //skye ba 'di nyid kho na la//bdag 'grub par byed pa'i mchog/dkyil 'khor du ni zhugs pa

156b4(rgyud): thub pa'i dkyil 'khor zhugs pa la//sdom pa ji ltar sbyin par bya//khyod kyis srog chags gsad mi bya//ma byin pa yang blang mi bya//'dod pas log par g.yem mi bya//brdzun du yang ni sgra mi bya//nyes pa kun gyi rtsa ba yi//chad dag 'thung ba spang bar 
156b5(rgyud): bya//bya ba ma yin thams cad dor//mchog tu sems can gdul bar bya//dam pa rnams la rim gro dang //rnal 'byor pa rnams bsnyen bkur bya//rnal 'byor rig pa bsgrub pa'i phyir//mkhas pa rnams la bsten par bya//lus kyi bas ni rnam gsum dang //

156b7(rgyud): mtshan ma'i phyag rgya gang yin pa//'gom pa nyid du mi bya zhing //bza' bar yang ni mi bya'o//de bzhin sman ma lha yi grib//rkang bdag gis brdzi mi bya//de yi steng du 'dug mi bya//slar yang gzhag par mi bya'o//lha yi phyag mtshan bzang po dang //
157a1(rgyud): @#//lha yi bzhon pa'i mtshan ma rnams//'gom zhing brnyas par mi bya ste//sems can la ni gnod mi bya//de yis srog gi phyir yang ni//'dor bar mi bya gang zhig yin//'tsho ba'i srog gi phyir yang ni/byang chub sems ni dor mi bya//rdo rje dril bu phyag rgya ni//nam 
157a2(rgyud): yang dor bar mi bya'o//dkon mchog gsum la dag pa yang //rnam pa kun tu gtang mi bya//sdom pa gtang bar mi bya'o//bdag nyid gdung bar mi bya'o//khrus kyi las ni ji ltar bya//mi dge ba ru ni spongs nas su//dge ba bcu ni sten byed cing //'byung po rnams la phan 'dod pa//nang gi 
157a3(rgyud): bdag nyid khrus su 'dod//bdag nyid rab tu dag bya'i phyir//gnas nyid phyag rgyas rgyas gdab phyir//phyag rgya thams cad yang dag bcings//rnal 'byor pa yi khrus su brjod//gsang ba'i khrus lbas len pa'i//chu yi khrus ni bya ba ste//las 'di rnam par rtog 
157a4(rgyud): pa'i khrus//rdo rje slob dpon dag gis bya//yang na tshogs khrus bya ba//mchog tu ngo mtshar bshad bya ste//dang po rab dag nyid go bgo zhing //bdag nyid bkra ba'i phyir du ni//khro bo yang ni dran par bya//don kun rab tu sgrub byed pa'i//yig 
157a5(rgyud): gnyis go cha bzang po yin//rdo rje gnod sbyin drag chen dang //khro bo bdud rtsi 'khyil ba ste//oM badzra kI li kI li badzra hU~M phaT/oM hU~M go cha'o//oM hU~M A rdo rje gnod sbyin gyi'o//oM a mr-i te hU~M phaT/bdud rtsi 'khyil ba'i 'o//rdo rje'i gsang sngags bdid dang ni//
157a6(rgyud): rdo rje 'bar ba'i phyag rgya yis//rdo rje 'bar rab bzod dka' bas//bdag nyid dag ni 'bar ba bya//oM d+hu pi d+hu pi kA ya d+hu pi tha dz+wa la ni swA hA/chu yi nang du 'jug pa dang //snyim pa gsum ni blugs pa dang //sangs rgyas kun gyi rigs dag la//rig 
157a7(rgyud): sngags 'di ni 'dod pa yin//rig sngags mngon par bzlas pa yi//sa yi phung po gsum dag ni//bdag gi stod dang smad dang dbus//rab tu dag byed mchog yin no//oM b+hU ra dza la hU~M/sangs rgyas thams cad rjes dran pas//rig sngags thams cad mngon bzlas

157b3(rgyud): par 'gyur//dga' ba chen po ji lta bu//rab tu gus bcas phyag dag bya//phyag rgya bzhi yis phyogs bzhi ru//lnga pa brtsegs pa'i phyag rgya yis//steng dang 'og dang mtshams rnams su//bstod dang bcas pas phyag bya'o//rdo rje slob dpon skal chen gyi//mchog tu gsang ba'i

181a1(rgyud): @#//po'i lcags kyu'i phyag rgya yis//phyag na rdo rje spyan drang bya//rdo rje lcags kyu'i sbyor ba yis//kun la nyi shu rtsa gcig bzlas//bsnyems tshul sngags kyi phyag rgya yis//rnam snang yang dag spyan drang bya//sangs rgyas lcags kyu'i sbyor ba yis //de bzhin

181a3(rgyud): nyid dbul ba la sogs pa'i//ji skad bshad pa'i brtul zhugs spyad//bshags dang gsol gdab mchod pa dang //bdag nyid dbul ba la sogs rnams//bdag gi bya ba rdzogs nas ni//phyag rgya bcing ba yang dag brtsam//brgya 'am yang na stong dag gam//
181a4(rgyud): brgya phrag brgya ni bsdus pa yis//phyag rgya'i tshogs ni bcing byas nas//dkyil 'khor dag ni 'grub par 'gyur//de nas dkyil 'khor las byas te//ji srid skya rengs shar gyur pa//de srid phyag rgya yang dag bcing //nges par dngos grub ster bar byed//de nas 
181a5(rgyud): langs te phyag rgya bcas//dkyil 'khor phyi rol byung nas ni//gdug pa la sogs kun bsdigs te//dkyil 'khor mchod phyir 'jug par bya//rang gi khrus byas lus 'dris byugs//ji ltar 'tsham pa'i rgyan gos ldan//shin tu mdzes pa'i gos gyon zhing //phreng ldan kha ni 
181a6(rgyud): dri zhim pas//rdo rje gar gyi sbyor ba yis//me tog spyi bor gzhag byas te//rol ba'i rdo rje dga' ba yis//zhugs nas mgon la rab btud cing //de nyid 'dod chags rjes chags dang //de nyid bskul ba'i phyag rgya yis//dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba//sangs rgyas kun

181b1(rgyud): phyag rgya yang dag bcing //de nas rdo rje slob dpon gyis//de nyid rdo rje lcags kyu bcing //steng du se gol brdabs pa yis//sangs rgyas thams cad bsdu bar bya//sangs rgyas bsdu ba'i sngags ni dza tsha dzaH//shar gyi sgo ru yi ge dzaH//de bzhin lho ru yi ge hUM//nub tu lcags 
181b2(rgyud): srog chen po baM//byang du kun du dbab pa yaH//rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor thams cad la//las 'di dag ni yang dag bshad//las ni kun du rdzogs byas nas//de bzhin ngo mtshar mthong 'gyur ba//cho 'phrul chen po rmad 'byung che//sna tshogs brgya dang rtsa bcu ste//cho 'phrul rmad
