182a2(rgyud): mo nyin mo mthong //nyi ma'i 'od kyis kun khyab pa'i//yang na nyi ma 'char ba ste//yang na zla ba'i dkyil 'khor ro//dag pa'i zhing ni mthong nas su//sangs rgyas rol pa mthong bar 'gyur//'dus pa shin tu dag pa yi//dbang bskur ba ni mthong bar 'gyur//

183b7(rgyud): lha dang lha min gsang ba pa//khyod kyis bzlas par mi bya zhing //phyag rgya bzhon pa mtshon cha dang //mtshan ma 'gom par mi bya'o//'di dag dam tshig yin par bshad//khyod kyis rtag tu bsrung bar bya//rjes mthun sdom pa rgya cher ni//gsang ba'i rgyud las rab

201b5(rgyud): bdug pa dang //dri yi tshogs ni yid 'ong dang //rin chen rang bzhin rgya mtsho ni//thub pa'i dbang phyug rnams la 'bul//de nas phyogs rnams thams cad du//me tog bdug pa la sogs sprin//rol mo'i tshogs ni yid 'ong ba//mchod pa'i sprin ni rnam par bsam//
201b6(rgyud): mchod pa thams cad rab byed pa//rig pa'i rgya chen 'di rnams dang //phyag rgya dag kyang yongs gsal ba'i//bsam pa yis ni gzung bar bya//oM sarba ta thA ga ta puSh+pa pU dzA me g+ha sa mu dra s+pha ra Na sa ma ye hUM/oM sarba 
201b7(rgyud): ta thA ga ta gan d+ha pU dzA me g+ha sa mu tra s+pha ra Na sa ma ye hU~M/gang du rgyal chen me tog dang //bdug pa dang ni dri yi tshogs//de bzhin kun du yang dag sbyar//than rgyan spel rnams kyang ngo //mchod pa'i phyag rgya tshul chen dang //phyag rgya chen po bslab par bya//
202a1(rgyud): @#//theg pa chen po bsdus pa las//ji skad rgya chen gsungs de bzhin//sems can thams cad bskyab pa dang //bde ba chen po'i rgyu dang ni//'gro ba gsum las dgrol ba'i phyir//de lta bu yi bsam pa bya//'dir ni lha rnams thams cad kyi//
202a2(rgyud): bsam med nye bar zhi ba dang //mi rnams sdug bsngal sna tshogs pa//rab tu mthar phyin de bzhin nyid//theg pa dman par 'dod byed pa//'khor ba rnam par gzhol ba'i phyir//rdo rje rnal 'byor bdag nyid ches//'di de bsam par bya ba yin//bdag gis brjod 
202a3(rgyud): nams stobs dag gis//bde ba thams cad tshogs kyi mchog/sems can thams cad bde chen ldan//ma lus par ni grub gyur cig/sems can thams cad de gyur cig/'byung po kun la byams par shog/dud 'gro thams cad 'jigs med shog/

202a5(rgyud): ba'i mdo sde ma lus pa//yang dang yang du bklag par bya//mi ldog byang chub sems dpa' ru//gcig tu 'gyur 'dod sems kyis bya//rdo rje rnal 'byor bla na med//rnal 'byor chen po bsgrub par bya//zla ba 'am yang na zla ba drug/lo gcig gam ni de las gzhan//
202a6(rgyud): rig pa'i brtul zhugs 'di 'drar bya//phyag rgya thams cad yang dag bcing //sangs rgyas rnams la nyin re bzhin//nges par lha bshos dbul bar bya//lha bshos snang ba ster byed pa//de bzhin rig pa'i lha bshos la//rdo rje gnod sbyin khro bo che//thams cad du ni rjes 
202a7(rgyud): dran bya//'ching ba'i sbyor ba'i cho ga ni//rdo rje khu tshur gyis brtan bya//rdo rje go cha'i sbyor ba yis//dkyil 'khor kun du bsrung bar bya//de lta bu yi cho ga yis//rnal 'byor bya ba bsgrub par bya//bsrung ba byas shing brtson 'grus brtsam//stobs chen spro ba 
202b1(rgyud): bskyed pa yis//nyin re zhing ni brtags nas su//rtog pa'i dkyil 'khor bsam par bya//sku mdog dkyil 'khor ji bzhin par//ji ltar 'os shing 'tsham pa ru//dgag pa la sogs las rnams ni//thams cad mnyam par gzhag par bya//ci nus 
202b2(rgyud): par ni nyin re zhing //rdo rje'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni//mtshams med lnga po byed pa yang //lo gcig na ni rab 'grub 'gyur//theg pa chen po'i tshogs dang na//mchog 'gyur rdo rje sems dpar 'gyur//rgyud gzhan gyi ni cho ga dang //mdo sde dang bcas ci bzhin du//
202b3(rgyud): de nas rig pa'i brtul zhugs blang //sdom pa dang dor bar mi bya zhing //kha na mtho thul tsam yang //brtsod pa ru ni bya ba yin//gnas ni yang dag brtag par bya//tsher ma la sogs dang bral ba//ri bo 'am ni dgon pa'am//lha khang skyed mos tshal dang ni//
202b4(rgyud): chu yi 'gram dang 'brog dgon par//gang du phyogs ni gtsang mar gyur//gseg ma kyo mo bong ba dang //rdo ba dag ni bsal bar bya//shin tu legs par dag bya ste//gang phyir lha rnams yongs su 'dzin//gal te de ni sbyor lhod gyur//rnam pa kun tu mi 'jigs 
202b5(rgyud): bya//sangs rgyas thams cad rgyal po che//chos kyi bdag po dgongs pa can//thugs rje snying por ldan gyur pa//sangs rgyas nyes par 'dzin mi 'gyur//gnas ni kun du dag bya zhing //dkyil 'khor yang ni ci nus par//rgya ru dpag tshad tsam bya ste//khru 
202b6(rgyud): drug las ni dma' ba min//sngon la phur bu gdab bya ste//rdo rje lta las sogs pa bcing //de nas yo byad thams cad ni//cho ga mchog gi slad par bya//rdo rje slob dpon rang nyid 'dir//dkyil 'khor dag ni bsgrub par bya//thal ba la sogs dus ji bzhin//
202b7(rgyud): mkhas pas dkyil 'khor brtag par bya//ji ltar nus shing spro ba dang //ji ltar rdzas ni 'byor pa dang //de bzhin rnal 'byor chen pos bya//yang na sems kyis brtag par bya//ci ste bsgrub pa rgyal por 'gyur//cho ga gzhan gyis blo ldan 
203a1(rgyud): @#//pas//gal te dngos grub mchog 'dod na//dkyil 'khor dag ni dag par bya//tshon rtsi lnga dang yang dag ldan//khru ni stong dang ldan pa mchog/yang na rgyang grags tsam du ni//sa yi cha ni thig gdab bya//gal te 'byor pa ldan pa min//hU~M 
203a2(rgyud): bzhi mdo ru bsdus par bya//yang na phyogs rnams thams cad du//khru brgyad dang ni yang dag ldan//in+d+ra nI la shin tu sngo//tshon rtsi sngon po rab tu bya//pad+ma rA ga shin tu dmar//tshon rtsi dmar po zhes byar bshad//rdo rje mchu dang mchu chen po//ser po yang ni yang dag 
203a3(rgyud): ldan//dag cing dad pa'i gser dang ni//kha dog ser po ldan par bya//shin tu dag ba shel dang bas//tshon rtsi dkar po rab tu bya//gang phyir rgyal po nor chen ldan//tshon rtsi 'di dag yang dag bshad//phyug cing nor dang yang dag ldan//gal te de ni khyim bdag gyur//
203a4(rgyud): khru brgya yongs su tshang bar bya//bcu drug pas ni nyung mi bya//tshon rtsi dri dang yang dag ldan//gur gum la sogs dang bsres pa//tshon rtsi lnga dang yang dag ldan//mkhas pa yis ni dkyil 'khor bya//phyug cing nor dang ya nga dag ldan//tshong dpon yang ni ded 
203a5(rgyud): dpon nam//khyim pa 'byor pa dang ldan pa//gzhan dag dga' zhing 'ongs gyur na//rin chen byi ma dang bsres pa'i//tshon rtsi chen po rab tu bya//rams dang bzla ba sogs pa//tshon rtsi lnga dag yang dag ldan//bdag nyid chung ba'am che ba'ang rung //de yi 'byor ba brtag 
203a6(rgyud): bya ba//ji ltar rigs par nges par sbyar//rgyal po la ni tshad bshad pa//khyim bdag la yang de bzhin no//tshong dpon dang ni ded dpon gyi//khru ni lnga bcu nyid kyang ngo //gzhan dag dga' zhing 'dongs gyur na//khru ni nyi shur byas pa yin//gal te dngos grub bsgrub 'dod na//
203a7(rgyud): khru drug las ni chud mi bya//mang po de bzhin nyung ba yang //dri dang kun du yang dag ldan//rtag tu sna tshogs dri yis ni//sngon dus gzhi dag par bya//phyi rol dkyil 'khor ngos dag gi//bA ni bzhi po thams cad du//shi sangs can gyis bdug spos bsreg/
203b1(rgyud): da phyir rdo rje can dag gshegs//de nas dkyil 'khor gnas de na//sngags kyis bsangs pa'i dri chu yis//rtag tu legs par chag chag gdab//gang phyir rdo rje'i rang bzhin 'gyur//nyin zhag drug gam bdun brgyad dang //mar dang 'o ma 'bras 
203b2(rgyud): ni nyid//slob dpon zas ni dkar gsum bza'//yang na thun mong gyur pa'i zas//ci ste bzod ldan nyin mtshan du//mi bza' bar ni bsgrub par bya//gang phyir chos kyi rang bzhin zas//stobs ldan 'gyur bar the tshom med//dkyil 'khor mchog ni rgyal chen po//

203b4(rgyud): mal kun du bri//yang na bcu bzhi la brtsams te//nyin zhag gnyis par yang dag bri//yang na bco lnga de yi ni//snga dro de yi yang dag brtsam//tshul chen po dang mtshungs pa yi//dkyil 'khor shes rab can gyis bri//gang gi tshe nas brtsams nas su//ji srid

203b6(rgyud): bkra shis pa'i//nyin zhag dag pa gang yin pa//de la rdo rje slob dpon ni//skal ba chen pos dkyil 'khor brtsam//sems dpa'i skyil mo krung bcas nam//dbus kyi cha ru 'dug par bya//sangs rgyas thams cad yang dmigs nas//sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa bsgom//
203b7(rgyud): sems ni yongs su 'dzin byed pa//lus ni drang por byas nas su//bshags la sogs pa'i las rnams ni//thams cad sngon du bya ba yin//byang chub sems la brtson gyur cig/rig pa'i brtul zhugs spyad pa yis//phyogs bcu ru ni phyag byas nas//bdag 
204a1(rgyud): @#//nyid kyang ni spro ba bskyed//deng bdag skye ba 'bras bur bcas//bdag gi 'tsho ba'ang 'bras bu yod//sangs rgyas rnams dang dam tshig gis//deng ni srid par mnyam par 'gyur//byang chub sems dpa' rtse gcig pa'i//sems kyis phyir mi

204a6(rgyud): nang //rdo rje'i gzugs dang ldan pa la//rnal 'byor pa yis snyoms 'jug bya//yang na rnal 'byor gang rung la//nus na snyoms par 'jug par bya//sems ni mnyam par gzhag 'gyur ba//go rims bzhin du bshad par bya//bcom ldan sngon ni ci 'dra ba'i//byang chub

204b1(rgyud): sgo song ba can gyis bskul ba bsam//sangs rgyas kun la phyag byas shing //ci 'dod pa ni bka' nod bya//nges par zla ba'i dkyil 'khor du//rang gi mgo bor brtag par bya//byang sems yang dag bskyed nas ni//dbus su zla ba brtag par bya//de nas rang gi ye

206a7(rgyud): grib bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//cha mnyam pas ni mdzes par gyur//dar gyi chun pos brgyan gyur cing //rin chen dra bas rnam par brgyan//g.yer ka'i dra bas yang dag ldan//kha dog lnga pos rab tu mdzes//dril bu'i dra bas shin tu spras//rnga yab sna tshogs 
206b1(rgyud): kyis mdzes pa//phreng ba rnga yab bla re dang //nyi zla dag gis nye bar mdzes//kun nas phyi rol gnas dag tu//rdo rje kha khyer yang dag rten//kun du kha khyer dang ldan pa//sgo ni kha khyer med pa'o//rtse mo rdo rje sngor rang bzhin//gzhan ni gser gyi mdog can dang //dmar po pad+ma
